# Issue #244 implementation evidence

Tracking issue: https://github.com/Ooples-Finance-LLC/OoplesFinance.StockIndicators/issues/244

Implementation is in progress. This ledger does not mark the library-wide assurance plan complete.

## Adopted compatibility decisions

- V2 shared validation requires formula references by default. `IndicatorValidationOptions.Smoke()` explicitly opts into universal checks without complete formula coverage.
- Startup values must be finite unless an indicator implements `IIndicatorStartupContract` with a per-output NaN policy. Infinity is never an unavailable-value marker. Post-warmup outputs must be finite.
- The new reference overload takes `IndicatorErrorBudget` and checks startup by default. The tolerance overload also checks from bar zero; it is not evidence that its generic tolerance is appropriate for every output. Sign-sensitive contracts must require matching signs, including nonzero versus zero.
- Batch multi-series evaluation requires equal counts and identical strictly increasing timestamps. Unsupported alignment throws before state reset/update. Callers must align observations explicitly; the library does not impute or truncate them.
- Benchmark previews do not replace committed cached benchmark values. Final primary evaluation consumes the committed benchmark; preview snapshots are not committed observations.
- Rolling population deviation remains the approved definition for `StandardDevation.Std`, with the selected moving average controlling Signal.
- Native RSMK requires positive finite closes on both inputs. Shared paired validation injects invalid observations, checks rejection, and compares recovery with fresh and reset trajectories. Customer domain declarations use the same path. Additional replay fixtures are now exercised by both validators.
- V2 streaming default outputs now agree with generated/fast defaults: `FastEdo` for EhlersDecyclerOscillatorV1 and `UpperBand` for VortexBands. Named outputs remain available.

## Baseline and hosted enforcement audit

The inherited working tree is preserved. A SHA-256 manifest of 653 tracked/untracked source files was captured at `C:/Users/cheat/temp/si-issue244-baseline.json`; HEAD alone does not identify this working tree.

Read-only GitHub API audit on 2026-09-23: repository rulesets returned no entries. `master` branch protection requires one approving review and code-owner review, dismisses stale reviews, and has strict status checks enabled, but its required check names and checks arrays are empty. Administrator enforcement is disabled. Hosted settings have not been changed during implementation. A workflow alone cannot establish mandatory enforcement.

## Executed checks

- First implementation batch: 4,233 tests passed, zero failures/skips. Includes shared customer and built-in validation and the new alignment/startup/sign regressions. TRX: `C:/Users/cheat/temp/si-issue244-results/issue244-s1.trx`.
- Exact-arithmetic pilot initially exposed two defects: SMA lost surviving small values after a `1e100` sample left the window; population deviation rounded the mean when the offset was `1e12` and spread less than one. Both have implementation fixes and retained regression cases; subsequent verification is recorded when complete.
- Independently varied period configurations exposed an oracle clamp-order error for reversed DAMA periods. The final slow-period cap is the existing accepted behavior; an explicit `[3,1,8,2]` identity case for fast=6/slow=1 now checks both calculation and reference.
- Historical integration fixtures used today's dates for benchmark data against unrelated historical primary dates. Valid-pair fixtures now reuse primary dates; dedicated misalignment tests assert rejection.

## Remaining completion gates

The plan's unchecked work remains required: complete formula provenance/variant review, per-output numerical/domain specifications across the live inventory, pilot expansion beyond SMA/EMA/population deviation, conditional extreme-input and lifecycle rollout, systematic invalid-input enforcement, full critical mutation campaign, platform/package verification and enforced release promotion. Deterministic generator/shrinker and verifier infrastructure do not by themselves satisfy these gates.

## Subsequent verification and corrections (2026-09-23)

- The independent pilot now includes RSI, RSMK, recursive filtering and mixed customer/built-in averages. The focused domain/pilot batch passed 90 tests (`issue244-domains.trx`).
- An expanded validation/component/integration run passed 17,042 of 17,052 tests. Its ten failures were retained and investigated: two default-output route mismatches; an obsolete, dimensionally incorrect Kase oracle; a too-short warmup fixture; a substitution sweep configuring every optional stage instead of the common average kind; changed last-bit rounding observations; and four inappropriate convergence assumptions. Fixes preserve the original route tolerance. Normalized decaying transients now receive independent trajectory checks; slowly converging statistics receive longer fixtures.
- Follow-up passed 864 of 865 tests (`issue244-route-repairs-2.trx`); the remaining failure identified four outputs that became bit-identical and were removed from the rounding-difference ledger. Final ledger verification is pending below.
- Critical mutation campaign 2 passed its baseline and killed all 16 reviewed faults. Evidence: `C:/Users/cheat/temp/si-issue244-mutations-2/evidence.json`. The earlier campaign had 14 kills and two compile-time inconclusive outcomes; those were not credited. Later compatibility/default-output edits postdate campaign 2's snapshot, so this evidence does not identify the final release source.
- All three library target frameworks build after removing unsupported reference APIs and correcting nullable dictionary metadata. The build reports existing dependency-support warnings for net461; runtime testing is separate from build success.
- The first local package's net10 DLL passed 5,525 configurations and its hash matched the package. Other target DLLs were stale because `GeneratePackageOnBuild` makes a standalone `pack` reuse builds; subsequent candidates explicitly build all targets before packing.
- Package execution on net461 exposed an existing cache-key defect: the `R` format was used for integer/decimal options. These now use exact `G` formatting, with floating types retaining `R`. A fresh package runtime run is in progress.
- CI/release now call a reusable critical-mutation workflow. Release promotion also requires regression tests, exact-package verification, all seven named platform artifacts, matching package/framework identity, and unfiltered evidence. Workflow YAML parsed successfully; hosted execution/enforcement remains unverified.

## Input-boundary and startup follow-up

- Candidate `2.0.0-issue244.local3`, SHA-256 `460AB4294514EE778BB9FD63A7E9EBF7DCE99270E6B5C0F4A139EFF94A91D764`, passed 5,525 configurations each on Windows net461 and net8. These runs predate the subsequent input/startup changes and are not final-source evidence.
- Typed finite/live paths now enforce finite raw fields and customer `IIndicatorInputDomainContract` declarations. Root rejection precedes state advancement; derived/update failures fault live graphs and do not publish partial histories. The input-boundary/component batch passed 126 tests. See [V2_INPUT_CONTRACTS.md](V2_INPUT_CONTRACTS.md).
- Native paired discovery independently varies each period as well as coupled periods. Both shared validators now generate field-wise invalid-input probes. Paired runs check state recovery; reports count actual rejections.
- Full unit run: 18,479 passed, two failed, no skips (`issue244-full-unit.trx`). The failures exposed a SlowEdo test reading the unnamed default and three empty unnamed fallback outputs. Corrections request SlowEdo explicitly and select fallback keys without collapsing multi-output generated types.
- Built-in references previously checked only after warmup despite startup finiteness checks. All now compare from bar zero. A public verifier run covered 5,547 configurations and exposed four failing configurations: QmaSmaDifference's reference omitted SMA zero seeding, and EhlersMarketState crossed its nine-degree decision boundary due to rounding. Both have pending corrected verification.
- EhlersMarketState uses a neutral ambiguity band of `1e-10` degrees below its strict nine-degree threshold. This is an explicit decision convention, not a claimed analytic angular error bound.
- Recurrence-only evidence is now identified separately from independent trajectories in `IndicatorFormulaCoverage` and verifier XML. Complete registration still does not establish independent trajectory coverage or mathematical proof.
- A long-period Butterworth oracle lost accuracy through partial-fraction cancellation during startup. Its reference now expands the transfer polynomial and convolves impulses in decimal, preserving startup corrections before final rounding. This reference remains limited by decimal range and double-precision transcendental coefficients.

## Runtime output boundary

- Typed finite/live/component paths now enforce finite outputs and declared startup NaN policies, with structured `IndicatorOutputException` diagnostics. Failure returns no finite run or faults a live run before partial histories are published. Finite failure disposes the runtime; custom finite states are disposed on completion or failure.
- The first output-boundary/customer/component batch passed 79 tests (`issue244-runtime-outputs.trx`). Subsequent direct startup-infinity and disposal regressions are in the pending library-wide rollout run.
- The mutation manifest now has 20 designated faults. Startup-infinity mutation targets the runtime policy because the runtime and validator now independently protect that boundary; changing only the validator loop no longer removes protection. Added a direct nonfinite-output-publication fault. Campaign 3's 19-fault snapshot predates these changes.
- Campaign 3 passed its baseline and killed all 19 faults (`si-issue244-mutations-3/evidence.json`, source snapshot `5f0282b3d305503bccecde8027d1a7b29fec7df58b5cd1046fea99acfd0b2416`). This is historical evidence for the pre-output-guard snapshot. Campaign 4 targets the updated 20-fault manifest.
- Output-guard rollout passed **5,628 tests, zero failures/skips** (`issue244-output-rollout.trx`), including every discovered single/paired built-in configuration. Subsequent builder/integration/warmup-disposal verification passed **326 tests, zero failures/skips** (`issue244-output-integration.trx`). Live warmup failures now dispose their states before propagating the exception.
- Candidate `2.0.0-issue244.local5`, SHA-256 `76E197A0F57A2BC89A4CEC039CD10B4C5A2229917A8825E93D8990520FD1852C`, passed all 5,547 configurations on Windows net10/net8/net461 with matching package DLL hashes. Campaign 4 passed its baseline and killed all 20 faults. Both predate the snapshot/default-primary changes below.

## Snapshot and default-output contracts

- Historical snapshots previously always claimed readiness; live readiness could mark an explicitly unavailable startup NaN as warmed. Snapshot readiness now includes dependency warmup, priming bars and actual output availability. Historical runs retain startup rows with accurate status; live runs suppress them unless explicitly requested.
- Snapshot/live indexers, chained sources and component inputs previously selected slot zero even when an indicator declared another primary output. They now use the same checked primary-output selection as finite runs. The shared validator automatically compares default series and latest snapshots with the declared slot on every fixture.
- Initial snapshot verification passed 382 tests and exposed the remaining live-indexer mismatch. After fixing all implicit-primary readers, 383 tests passed (`issue244-snapshot-contracts-2.trx`), including opposite-signed primary/secondary values through finite/live snapshots, chaining and composition.
- Three new status/mapping faults bring the designated mutation manifest to 23. Campaign 5 and the final automatic primary-output rollout are pending. Earlier snapshots are not substituted for this evidence.

## SMA overflow and cancellation batch

- Added an exact fixed-point accumulator for SMA windows whose running sum overflows or suffers detected severe cancellation. It rounds the rational mean once, nearest-even, and preserves preview/reset. The [derivation and limits](V2_FORMULA_VARIANTS.md#simple-moving-average-finite-range-and-cancellation) distinguish the fallback from ordinary floating arithmetic.
- Overflow verification passed 904 tests (`issue244-sma-overflow.trx`). Cancellation and all discovered built-in/component/streaming verification passed **6,772 tests, zero failures/skips** (`issue244-sma-cancellation.trx`). Subsequent mandatory-fixture/rational-reference/rounding verification passed 92 tests (`issue244-sma-mandatory-extremes.trx`); all three route-parity sweeps passed (`issue244-sma-route-parity.trx`).
- SMA configurations now receive all ten numerical fixture classes automatically. Other indicators still need conditional extreme-class rollout. Its reference uses separate rational window arithmetic with a relative-only budget and exact sign/zero agreement. Three SMA fault seeds bring the manifest to 32; their campaign is pending. Mutation execution disables package generation because it tests net10, while the exact-package job separately executes the framework matrix.


## ROMA source correction and independent evidence

- Authenticated alexgrover's original April 26, 2019 ROMA source: its gain uses smoothed absolute error over the rolling maximum of that same smoothed error. Corrected the streaming numerator; core component evaluation now uses the same state/startup semantics. Independent validation now predicts its own full feedback history using recomputed centered regression windows. Hand-derived period-four, bounded-reduction, preview/reset and corrupted-output tests cover the distinction. Verification is pending; no completion claim yet.
- Strict shared validation now rejects recurrence-only evidence. Startup coverage must come from an independent reference; combining a startup residual with a post-warmup independent trajectory cannot satisfy it. Added customer regressions; verification pending.

## Correctly rounded weighted arithmetic and numerical release evidence

- EMA now rounds the complete integer-weighted recurrence once, preserving subnormal constants and cancellation residues. Its independent rational correction-form trajectory is compared exactly. All eleven numerical classes are automatic for EMA. The EMA/shared-arithmetic batch passed **135 tests** (`issue244-ema-recurrence.trx`).
- WMA and its LinearWeightedMovingAverage alias now keep exact rolling ordinary/weighted sums through insertion and eviction, with one final rounding and the existing zero-padded startup. The legacy linear alias uses the corrected kernel. Independent rational windows and both aliases receive every numerical class automatically. Combined focused verification passed **195 tests** (`issue244-weighted-means.trx`). Full-suite and five-mutation results are pending.
- All **45** critical mutation sites match the current source. The former EMA coefficient and initialization sites were updated for the new arithmetic; their previous archived campaign outcomes remain unchanged.
- A separate numerical publication gate now requires completed, passing, nonempty executed evidence for every required generated numerical fixture on every discovered configuration. The package verifier records the generator's current required fixture names. New classes become required automatically; removing the established eleven from a report cannot weaken the floor. Formula-only or old-schema reports cannot pass this gate. Domain rejection counts remain distinct from formula values evaluated on recovery inputs.
- This gate deliberately blocks publication while the numerical rollout is incomplete. It is not evidence that the remaining indicators already satisfy those classes; hosted enforcement is also still separate from these unmerged workflow changes.

- Weighted-arithmetic full run: **18,810 passed / 9 failed** (`issue244-full-weighted-means.trx`). One failure correctly demanded deletion of the now-bit-identical linear-WMA tolerance exception. Eight reports (two suites, four configurations) found Elder Impulse's reference still using the old EMA product-rounding contract. Updated that independent reference to its own rounded rational trajectory; discrete assertions were not relaxed. Migration notes now call out possible near-zero signal changes.
- Five-fault subset 14 caught **5/5 selected faults**, selectedComplete=true, full complete=false, snapshot `2631a9447f6ff025f575ad55b18742ba4332c3586c98e8b1686e5d0778050182`, archive `378b72d6f92326962ed1267c12881820d14291691de20dcc6f1854e4afb2d08e`. This predates the small-integer optimization.
- A native 100,000-bar probe exposed excessive allocations in the initial exact path: EMA ~204MB, WMA ~264MB per run. Added a checked signed-integer representation with wide fallback and exact quotient/remainder rounding. Focused verification, including the corrected Elder reference and route ratchet, passed **200 tests** (`issue244-weighted-small-path.trx`). The optimized probe reports EMA 10.517ms/144 bytes, WMA 7.497ms/560 bytes versus the initial exact path 82.909ms/204MB and 110.077ms/264MB. Baseline local14 was .767ms/128 bytes and 6.649ms/520 bytes; full-suite activity affected earlier timings, so these are diagnostic observations, not competitor evidence or a final performance claim. Raw data and DLL hashes are in `si-issue244-weighted-cost`.
- All **10 tool tests passed**, including complete/missing/failed/empty/duplicate/old-schema numerical release evidence and newly added required classes. The numerical publication gate remains intentionally unsatisfied library-wide.

- Optimized weighted-arithmetic full suite passed **18,823 tests, zero failures/skips**, 6m43s (`issue244-full-weighted-small-path.trx`). This predates the native input guards.
- Subset 15 caught **7/7 selected faults**, baseline passed, selectedComplete=true, full complete=false. Snapshot `a2d3d68266ac6361d8330aafec30166d009a3ac3ecc5ef739daf509b46a515a5`, archive `e4fa87984095573b87edc9d60fac56d4b2469b5ab605188b4a97be68551366c6`. It verifies the optimized arithmetic snapshot, not the newer input guards.

## Native rejection and population-deviation range coverage

- Shared native input resolution now rejects nonfinite OHLCV and custom-selector values before indicator state updates. Scalar EMA rejects them on its period-one identity path as well. **246 focused tests passed**, zero failures/skips (`issue244-native-input-rejection.trx`), including 123 new preview/commit/recovery cases. Native states that bypass this resolver require their own route coverage; this is not a claim about every native state.
- Population deviation now falls back to exact integer moments when the translated floating variance overflows or its squared differences enter the subnormal range. In units of 2^-1074, it computes `A=N*sum(x*x)-sum(x)^2`, then rounds `sqrt(A)/N` on the binary64 grid. The midpoint comparison uses `4*A` versus `divisor^2*(2*q+1)^2`, including ties to even. The ordinary two-pass path remains; this is not an exact-rounding claim for that path.
- The independent oracle instead centers exact rational values and bisects binary64 encodings using rational square comparisons. StandardDevation now automatically receives all eleven numerical classes. Both Std and Signal use zero absolute tolerance with sign preservation; the selected average still controls Signal only. Verification pending.

- Population-deviation test build initially caught a missing required average-kind constructor argument; corrected without production edits. Reusing the built production references, **240 focused tests passed**, zero failures/skips (`issue244-population-extremes-2.trx`). This includes independent square-root midpoint/overflow tests, all eleven numerical classes with three signal averages, native/builder/legacy parity, reset and preview. Full shared-dependency suite and subset 16 mutation evidence are pending.

- Local15 SHA-256 `A88421EADDD868F3400F904BA8110010A80EE0F6110B87A878BA9589D3C6E859` passed all **5,547 configurations on Windows net10/net8/net461**. It includes weighted arithmetic, native guards, population deviation and required-numerical-fixture metadata; it predates order-statistic edits. The numerical gate correctly rejected the real report: only **48/5,547** configurations have all eleven required numerical classes; 60,489 class receipts remain absent. Passing discovered formula configurations is not completion of that gate.
- Subset 16 caught **7/7 selected faults**, baseline passed, selectedComplete=true, full complete=false. Snapshot `03afdbd0f5bc9d53e70d727d196cfb23763518faf091f5e2aaac106495322e4c`, archive `6e1ec0c96d27785ce834ca24ecab2e4d5ad5149d7088c38d69fc4e13954907da`. This predates order-statistic work.
- Full-suite verification found OBV now rejects its infinite intermediate at the scalar EMA guard before output validation. The output-policy test now uses direct range overflow to retain its exact exception/slot/bar assertions; a separate OBV regression requires the intermediate rejection and offending value. An attempted test-only build hit a Windows file lock from the active full suite and must be rerun after it exits.
- Next numerical batch covers highest/lowest, rolling max/min, percent rank, median and trimean. Median/trimean averages use the existing guarded finite-mean primitive. Percent rank forms its exactly representable integer percentage numerator before the single final division. Automatic class receipts and mixed-OHLC route/preview/reset tests are added; verification pending.

- Full population/guard suite finished **19,017 passed / 1 failed**, zero skipped, 11m42s (`issue244-full-population-extremes.trx`). The single failure was the OBV assertion described above. ## Automatically discovered native input rejection

- Initial typed-factory probe was unsuitable: 1,007 failures were unsupported typed factory mappings, not demonstrated native defects. Replaced it with reflection discovery of every public concrete `IStreamingIndicatorState` class and declared constructor defaults/minimum periods. Required constructor arguments fail visibly unless registered; CustomInputState now has explicit stateless-selector and stateful-series constructions.
- Direct native discovery exercised **824 classes / 1,648 configurations**. The first native run had 1,506 passing and 142 failing cases: 136 accepted invalid fields, four advanced state before rejection, and two required the wrapper's construction registration. Raw evidence is `issue244-discovered-native-rejection-2.trx`.
- Added entry validation to 146 Update methods that did not begin with the shared resolver, plus CustomInputState before its source series can advance. The remaining methods already validate via the resolver as their first statement. The wrapper also rejects a nonfinite selected value before calling its inner state. The discovered tests compare control trajectories after every rejected event through a recovery window; they automatically include future public native state classes. Verification pending. Arbitrary customer callbacks/series are still responsible for their own internal side effects.
- All **60** current critical mutation sites match; no final full-manifest campaign is claimed.


## Field-sensitive numerical fixtures and volume means

- Native entry guard verification completed: **1,928 passed**, no failures/skips (`issue244-native-entry-guards.trx`). Count-indicator numerical coverage completed **1,678 tests** (`issue244-count-numerics.trx`). These are overlapping focused results, not a new full-suite total.

The release evidence floor requires all 18 classes.

This predates the count/field/volume changes and contains only the old eleven-class receipts (84 configurations); it does not satisfy the current numerical release gate.
- VWAP and the periodic Bartlett windowed volume mean now retain exact binary products and total weights until the final ratio. Typical price is independently rounded once before weighting. All core, legacy, builder and native routes share this contract; selected chained input bypasses typical-price construction. Zero total volume retains zero output. Signed volume retains algebraic behavior and can yield an unrepresentable result; no convexity guarantee applies to signed weights.
- Added randomized rational product/ratio comparisons, explicit intermediate overflow/underflow, copied previews, reset, all 18 fixture classes, all discovered configurations for these two names, and custom chained input regression. Mutation subset111 killed **3/3** faults: floored odd lag, blanked startup and committed previews.

- LSMA now combines exact rounded WMA/SMA stages without intermediate overflow. Its core and public LsmaCore registry use the published zero-padded WMA/full-window SMA startup; six regression-based core consumers explicitly retain their old LinearRegression fit. Added the missing streaming factory registration. **11 focused/enrollment tests**, **4/4 public configurations**, and **2,638 shared checks** passed (`issue244-lsma.trx`, `si-issue244-lsma-public-evidence.xml`, `issue244-lsma-shared.trx`). Mutation subset112 killed **2/2** faults: intermediate overflow and reinstated partial-window regression.
- Executed post-LSMA inventory: **7,077** configurations/reference registrations, **2,253** numerical enrollment, **4,824** missing enrollment, **50** multi-series configurations, no construction failures (DLL `5A7339ADBE993B18782391107AD04817E20B17EA5A71628177F49085E626577F`).

- Simplified LSMA now evaluates its exact zero-padded OLS endpoint using a constant-time integer-weight recurrence, replacing overflow-prone double cumulative totals and fixing the core's different 2*WMA-SMA formula. Added both native factory registrations and an independent rational window reference. **13 focused/enrollment checks**, **1 selected-input check**, **4/4 public configurations**, and **2,638 shared checks** passed (`issue244-simplified-lsma.trx`, `issue244-simplified-lsma-selected.trx`, `si-issue244-simplified-lsma-public-evidence.xml`, `issue244-simplified-lsma-shared.trx`). Mutation subset113 killed **3/3** faults: retained expired tap, incorrect endpoint weight and committed previews. All **289** mutation sites apply; a full-current campaign remains outstanding.
- Executed post-Simplified-LSMA inventory: **7,077** configurations/reference registrations, **2,257** numerical enrollment, **4,820** missing enrollment, **50** multi-series configurations, no construction failures (DLL `283A27B9C5F5DB744EEF4C587968159D14892004C4A61F7D2F0BF06DABE21C6B`).

- Variance now publishes once-rounded population variance around the exact window mean, with constant-time exact rolling sums/squares. This removes translation bias (including `[1e16, 1e16+2]`), intermediate overflow and subnormal rounding loss across native, legacy and core routes. Added the streaming factory registration and an independent rational centered-deviation reference. **78 focused/population-deviation/enrollment checks**, **1 expanded rounding/recovery check**, and **4/4 public configurations** passed (`issue244-variance.trx`, `issue244-variance-ties.trx`, `si-issue244-variance-public-evidence.xml`). Shared verification passed **2,637** checks and identified the obsolete `Variance.Variance` tolerance exception; removing it passed the **1 exact-agreement guard** (`issue244-variance-shared.trx`, `issue244-variance-exact-agreement.trx`). Mutation subset114 killed **3/3** faults: sample divisor, retained expired square and committed previews. All **292** mutation sites apply; the full-current campaign remains outstanding.
- Executed post-Variance inventory: **7,077** configurations/reference registrations, **2,261** numerical enrollment, **4,816** missing enrollment, **50** multi-series configurations, no construction failures (DLL `3163F4EA87153380BAE8AC3E4D1901909DB9CD68991BEEEB2E18B3FA9159FA81`).

- StandardError and StandardErrorCore now compute exact rolling centered moments and round only the final square root: residual RMS for the fitted line, or population SEM. Added both streaming factory registrations and fixed selected/chained input in the SEM builder arm. - Raw linear regression and six aliases now use exact partial-window OLS with independently rounded endpoint, forecast, slope and series-origin intercept. Direct regression consumers use the same helper across routes. Added independent rational references, both native factories, all numerical classes, signed output-overflow rejection, selected-input, cancellation, expiry and preview/reset checks. Closed **24** backlog configurations. Four backlog configurations closed; mutation subset117 passed its baseline and killed **3/3** selected faults.

- StandardDeviationChannel now centers all routes on partial-window OLS, including the standalone core that incorrectly returned a moving average. Each band rounds the exact fitted endpoint plus/minus the multiplier times the rounded full-window population deviation, avoiding overflow in cancellable intermediate components. Added independent rational band references, factory registration and input/preview/reset/overflow checks. **2,650 combined focused/shared checks**, **4/4 public configurations** and **4 direct multiplier tests** passed (`issue244-regression-channel.trx`, `si-issue244-regression-channel-public-evidence.xml`, `issue244-regression-channel-multipliers.trx`). Four backlog configurations closed; mutation subset118 passed its baseline and killed **3/3** selected faults; its snapshot predates the additional multiplier tests.

- Bollinger bands, middle alias, percent-B and width use exact mean/deviation stages and once-rounded band/ratio arithmetic. Added both factory routes, selected-input/preview/reset/overflow checks, and **38** explicit average-component configurations (19 kinds at two periods). Closed **17** backlog configurations. Initial tests caught a missing namespace import, mean-stage rounding and single-output reference-key errors; corrected **2,676 focused/shared checks** and **55/55 public configurations** passed (`issue244-bollinger-corrected.trx`, `si-issue244-bollinger-public-evidence.xml`). - Skewness now evaluates the population third central moment from exact rolling integer moments and rounds only its signed square-root ratio. - Coverage throughput: added `tools/NumericalBacklogScanner`, which runs the existing public validator against a compiled library with bounded concurrent workers and durable per-case JSONL evidence. It forces all numerical shapes without changing production enrollment or error budgets; evidence identity prevents stale resume. Each shape also runs across calendar boundaries and extends 32 bars beyond warmup (minimum 256, maximum 8192). `tools/summarize_numerical_backlog.py` exports review queues and groups failure signatures, refusing incomplete/duplicate/inconsistent results. Eight CLI/summary regressions passed. Initial 256-bar triage scanned 745 representative types in 53.9 seconds and 392 follow-up configurations in 12.5 seconds, but its candidate counts are superseded: review exposed warmup/calendar blind spots, so final scanner v3 reruns the full 4,291-configuration backlog with stronger fixtures. Passing scans are candidates, not enrollment or release-gate evidence. Next implementation batches should group related formulas/shared arithmetic, use one build, and consolidate route/fault verification; do not return to a build-and-push cycle for every small family.

A hand example yields `1, 1/2, 1/3`; a separate Vostro example distinguishes thresholds 8 and 0 at normalized upper price 0.25. **1,541/1,541** combined family/enrollment/live tests passed (`trend-pattern-corrected.trx`), followed by **388/388** strengthened trend-family tests (`trend-pattern-strengthened.trx`). Mutation170 passed its **221**-test baseline and killed **6/7** faults; the Vostro threshold fault survived. Added the hand example; mutation171 passed its **2**-test baseline and killed that fault. No kill is credited for its original survival. All **492** manifest sites applied; all **7** final implementation/test/manifest files matched mutation171's archive after newline normalization. Inventory: **3,048** numerical enrollments, **4,073** omissions, **7,121** configurations. DLL `01E921B5E030C47CAED6926CC35744A10AA985D598ACEE9FE7DE57C2DBA88990`. Together, the ordinal, Natural/breadth and trend/pattern batches close **218** omissions across **41** types and detect **21** distinct faults across separately archived campaigns. Library-wide coverage and full-current release gates remain incomplete.
- Follow-up triage remains uncredited: EMA Wave/Reversal Points fail signed-maximum fixtures; the former can overflow an unpublished residual before a representable smoothed result. The 74-configuration Ehlers route probe (`ehlers-candidates-probe.trx`, 444 tests) exposed missing routes, startup NaNs in native Signal-to-Noise V2, Market State disagreement on ordinary inputs, and additional signed-maximum filter/adaptive failures. Passing bulk scans alone did not reveal these gaps. Reproduction harness is preserved locally as `si-ehlers-candidate-probe.cs`; no failing probe was enrolled or placed in the passing regression suite.

- Spectrum/Restoring Pull/Gaussian/FM batch: enrolled **38** configurations across **4** types. The shared spectrum engine and independent complex-trajectory reference now normalize each price prefix before differences and channel powers, retain a common scale for filter history, and leave previews uncommitted. Scale examples span `1e-200` through maximum double, abrupt scale changes and a resolved 17-bar sinusoid. Corrected signal comparisons across scale changes, missing typed/live bindings, Restoring Pull's selected-price fast route, and finite-prefix smoothing after an independently proven published overflow. **1,833/1,833** combined regressions and **4/4** dependent tuned-bypass/single-channel checks passed (`ehlers-bank-final.trx`, `ehlers-bank-dependents.trx`). Mutation173 passed **193** baseline tests and killed **5/5** faults; mutation174 passed **8** and killed **6/6**. Mutation172 stopped on an ambiguous site before any test evidence; the site was repaired for mutation174. Mutation175 passed its one-test baseline and killed the repaired customer-fixture bypass fault. The campaigns have separate archives because tooling/manifest corrections occurred between starts; production/test implementation is the same. All **16** final files except the subsequent tooling-test addition match mutation175's archive after newline normalization. **28** tooling tests passed, then the strengthened **7** mutation-tooling tests passed.
- Mutation-gate repair: the runner previously prevalidated only selected sites despite its all-sites comment. It now validates the complete manifest before building, including unselected faults. Repaired the stale customer-fixture target; the tooling test explicitly establishes that all **503** current sites apply uniquely, and a CLI regression proves a stale unselected site blocks execution. Earlier selected campaigns alone must not be treated as evidence of complete-manifest applicability. Inventory: **3,086** numerical enrollments, **4,035** omissions, **7,121** configurations. Added missing typed/live bindings, ordinary/tiny/maximum-scale hand examples, unclamped selected-input checks and independently proven Mayer output-overflow rejection. - OHLC volatility batch: enrolled **21** configurations across Close-to-Close, Parkinson, Garman-Klass, Rogers-Satchell and Yang-Zhang. Replaced quotient-before-log arithmetic in batch/core/native routes, preserved selected prices in three direct arms, added typed/live bindings, retained negative-variance clamping and independently specified Yang-Zhang's omitted zero-denominator residuals. A strict minimum-period case exposed log-rounding error amplified by variance: near-unity logs now use exact rational Taylor bounds, publishing only when both bounds round identically. Independent fixed-point logarithms remain the oracle. **371** checks passed in the rounded-log run; its only failure was a new test incorrectly rejecting the exactly zero one-return population variance. Corrected that test; **22/22** focused contract/golden checks passed, followed by **8/8** strengthened range and existing regression checks. Evidence: `ohlc-volatility-rounded-log.trx`, `ohlc-volatility-final-corrections.trx`, `ohlc-volatility-range-regressions.trx`. The logarithm change also passed LogReturns, logarithmic-volatility and Historical Percentile regressions in the first selection.

- True Strength family: enrolled **52** configurations across Tsi, TrueStrengthIndex and ErgodicTrueStrengthIndex V1/V2. - ZScore, FastZScore, InverseFisherZScore and InverseFisherFastZScore: enrolled **20** configurations across **4** types. Classical SMA Z-score normalizes the exact window mean/residual against exact population variance before final square-root rounding; selected other/customer means supply the numerator while variance remains on the original prices. ### Ehlers correlation numerical contracts

- Trend, Cycle, Angle and Market State add **16 configurations / four types**. The zero-padded Pearson window uses exact integer moments and normalizes before rounding; opposite finite extremes, subnormals and large-offset adjacent prices retain their correlations. Trend uses the descending lag ramp; cycle uses binary64 cosine/negative-sine samples with a neutral imaginary coordinate for periods one and two. The phase-axis suppression, monotonic hold and strict market-state boundary conventions are preserved.
- An independent rational centered-moment oracle uses binary64-encoding bisection for square-root rounding. Trend/cycle and discrete market-state outputs require exact equality; angles allow 1e-12 degrees absolute and 1e-14 relative error. Ideal-axis hand calculations remain separately checked to 12 decimal places. The new kernel is confined to this family; competitor performance qualification remains outstanding.
- Batch, core, direct raw arms, native and live states share the corrected kernel. Live registrations now cover all four indicators. Tests exercise selected input precedence, zero padding, every invalid OHLCV field without advancing state, alternate previews and reset/replay.
- **132/132** grouped and existing regression checks passed (`ehlers-correlation-final.trx`). Generated route checks were subsequently tightened to the declared per-output budgets; **87/87** follow-up checks passed, including existing golden tests and enrollment guards (`ehlers-correlation-strict.trx`). ### Kendall and logistic correlation contracts

- **Eight configurations / two types** now use independent rational references and numerical fixtures. Kendall tau-a compares prices with once-rounded partial-window OLS endpoints, retaining one extra exponent bit when the endpoint overflows. Ties contribute zero and missing history remains zero-padded. Integer comparisons avoid overflowing subtraction, and exact division publishes the bounded pair score. Its unused Pearson calculation is removed.
- Logistic Correlation uses exact rolling price moments against local consecutive time indices. Normalization happens before rounding; neither large offsets nor subnormal changes erase the correlation. The existing exponent ceiling of 100 is preserved, including its nonzero lower floor. Signed and zero gains remain supported; nonfinite gains are rejected. The rational centered-moment oracle uses independent square-root bisection and a fixed-point exponential. Kendall requires exact equality; logistic allows 4e-15 relative error, zero absolute error and matching sign.
- Batch, direct raw, native and live routes cover selected inputs, repeated previews, reset/replay and rejection of every invalid OHLCV field without advancing state. The new Kendall raw arm is tested directly and remains outside the served-arm qualification list. There are no separate internal core implementations for these two indicators.

### Growing mutation catalogs stay within the CI matrix limit

- The retained fault catalog exceeded the old 2,560-site ceiling, causing `--list-shards` to fail before CI could execute any faults. The planner now targets ten faults per shard and caps the matrix at 256 jobs, distributing larger catalogs across those jobs without omitting sites.
- All **29 tooling tests passed**, including boundary sizes 2,560/2,561, the current 2,717-entry working catalog, and 10,000 entries. Partition assertions require every ID exactly once, nonempty balanced shards, and at most 256 jobs. Current matrix generation succeeds with 256 jobs. This repairs scheduling; it does not constitute a full mutation-gate run.

A temporary diagnostic collected the two later mismatches after AMA; that diagnostic was removed before final verification. No failed diagnostic run is credited as passing. Coverage inventory and mutation counts are unchanged.


### ChopZone numerical contracts

- ChopZone retains rounded extended range, scale and normalized-slope stages before calculating a signed whole-degree angle. The typical price is an exact mean of original candle fields unless an explicit selected source replaces it. Native/live registration and customer smoothing preserve the separate range and smoothing periods. The inverse tangent avoids overflow in the former squared-slope expression.
- **50/50 focused tests passed** (`chopzone-numerical.trx`). 47 family checks,2 enrollment checks and1 complete hand-example test passed. Coverage includes five routes, positive/negative hand angles, four smoothers, tiny/extreme inputs, recovery, selected prices, callbacks, preview/reset and both shared invalid-input rejection cases. The initial test build rejected an attempted assignment to read-only Bar.Close; reconstructing the bar fixed that test-only error, and verification reused the unchanged production binaries. Fourteen new faults plus the existing native-rejection-after-child-mutation fault are caught. The existing fault was adapted to the new state implementation and exercised with two focused shared rejection cases; its old site was not silently discarded.

### Super Passband retains the DC zero and exact rolling RMS

- Super Passband applies the price difference before its two recursive poles and retains extended intermediate values. Its upper and lower bands use an exact sum of squares and a correctly rounded square root with partial-window startup. History grows with observed bars, and batch, core, typed and streaming routes share the calculation.
- **104/104 focused tests passed** (`super-passband-541.trx`). Twelve configurations cover an independent rational reference, a hand impulse, equal-gain cancellation, constant-price transients, extreme and subnormal prices, normalized maximum periods, all outputs and selected inputs, preview/reset and rejected-field atomicity. Focused verification includes existing Ehlers secondary-output regressions and the strengthened Roofing V1 level-reversal regression. Fourteen compiled behavioral faults are caught from two passing isolated baselines with matching source snapshots.

### Modified RSI retains roofing ratios and both recursive outputs

- Modified RSI retains signed-exponent roofing stages through its matched gain/absolute-change window. This preserves ratios determined by subnormal inputs and decaying roofing tails. Exact polynomial coefficients preserve the low-pass gain, both recursive outputs share the same arithmetic across all routes, and lazy history avoids allocation proportional to an unconsumed period. A verified direct fast arm now serves both output keys.

### Squelch preserves phase cycles and directional signals across price scales

- Squelch retains signed-exponent lag differences and in-phase/quadrature recursions through phase measurement.
